<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement a detailed plan titled "Frozen Plan-Start Score + Queue Execution Lifecycle" for the desloppify Python CLI tool. The core concept: scores become frozen snapshots from plan creation, users work through the entire queue, then scan to reveal updated scores. Scanning mid-queue shows a warning and requires attestation. The lifecycle is: scan → plan → execute queue → scan (score reveal) → repeat.

   The plan has 5 parts:
   - **Part A**: New helper `queue_progress.py` with plan-aware queue counting and frozen score display
   - **Part B**: Frozen plan-start score snapshot (schema, scan workflow, replace score displays in fix/resolve/review/next/status commands)
   - **Part C**: Scan queue gate with warning + attestation (new preflight, CLI flags, wire into scan)
   - **Part D**: Score reveal on post-queue scan (enhanced display, clear plan_start_scores after reveal)
   - **Part E**: Fix checklist text in apply_flow.py

   After implementing, the user also requested: "once you're done, can you sense-check the whole thing holistically? Is it excellently engineered? Is it robust? Is there any way you can actually run through all the various scenarios?"

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Frozen score snapshots stored in `plan.json` as `plan_start_scores`
   - Plan-aware queue counting (respecting skips, collapsing clusters)
   - Score lifecycle: scan → plan → execute → scan (reveal)
   - Queue gate pattern (modeled on existing `review/preflight.py`)
   - Attestation-based bypass for force-rescan
   - Transient state stashing (`_plan_start_scores_for_reveal` on state dict) for cross-phase communication
   - `QueueBuildOptions` dataclass for configuring work queue builds
   - `ScoreSnapshot` named tuple from state_mod
   - `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS` tuple for consistent error handling
   - The on-disk schema.py has `PLAN_VERSION = 2` (simpler than what git status showed as staged changes)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`** (NEW - Part A)
     - Core helper module for the entire feature
     - Contains `plan_aware_queue_count()`, `get_plan_start_strict()`, `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`, `print_execution_or_reveal()`
     ```python
     def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
         from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
         result = build_work_queue(
             state,
             options=QueueBuildOptions(
                 status="open", count=None, plan=plan,
                 collapse_clusters=True, include_skipped=False,
             ),
         )
         return result["total"]

     def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
         if not plan:
             return None
         return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")

     def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan: dict, queue_remaining: int) -> None:
         scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
         strict = scores.get("strict")
         if strict is None:
             return
         print(colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100", "cyan"))
         print(colorize(
             f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
             " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.", "dim",
         ))

     def print_execution_or_reveal(state: dict, prev, plan: dict | None) -> None:
         from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
         if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
             try:
                 remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
             except Exception:
                 remaining = 0
             if remaining > 0:
                 print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
                 return
         print_score_update(state, prev)
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`** (Modified - Part B1)
     - Added `plan_start_scores: dict` to `PlanModel` TypedDict
     - Added `"plan_start_scores": {}` to `empty_plan()` return
     - Added validation in `ensure_plan_defaults()`: `if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict): plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}`
     - NOTE: On-disk version has `PLAN_VERSION = 2`, no epic/synthesis fields

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`** (Modified - Parts B2, D2)
     - Added score snapshot logic after auto-cluster block:
     ```python
     # Snapshot scores at plan-start (first scan of a new cycle)
     if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
         scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
         if scores.strict is not None:
             plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
                 "strict": scores.strict, "overall": scores.overall,
                 "objective": scores.objective, "verified": scores.verified,
             }
             dirty = True

     # Clear plan_start_scores after a score-reveal scan (queue was empty)
     if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
         from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
         try:
             if plan_aware_queue_count(runtime.state, plan) == 0:
                 # Stash for the score-reveal display in scan_reporting_summary
                 runtime.state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
                 plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
                 dirty = True
         except Exception:
             pass
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/fix/apply_flow.py`** (Modified - Parts B3, E1)
     - Replaced `from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update` with `from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import print_execution_or_reveal`
     - Changed score display call to load plan and use `print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, _fix_plan)`
     - Changed checklist text from "Did score improve as expected?" to "Run `desloppify scan` to update state and refresh findings."

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`** (Modified - Part B3)
     - Replaced `print_score_update` import with `print_execution_or_reveal`
     - Replaced `_print_score_movement(...)` call with plan load + `print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, _resolve_plan)`
     - Replaced `print_score_update(state, prev)` in `cmd_ignore_pattern` similarly
     - Removed `_print_score_movement` from render imports
     - Linter auto-renamed `has_review_findings` to `_has_review_findings`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py`** (Modified - Part B3)
     - Replaced `print_score_update` import with `print_execution_or_reveal`
     - Changed `print_score_update(state, prev, config=config)` to plan load + `print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, _import_plan)`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/merge.py`** (Modified - Part B3)
     - Same pattern: replaced `print_score_update` with `print_execution_or_reveal`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`** (Modified - Part B4)
     - Updated `show_empty_queue()` signature to accept `plan_start_strict` and `target_strict` kwargs
     - When plan_start_strict is set and queue is empty, shows before/after comparison:
     ```python
     def show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict, *, plan_start_strict=None, target_strict=None):
         if queue.get("items"):
             return False
         if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
             delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
             delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
             print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
             print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Current: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}", "cyan"))
             print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to finalize your updated score.", "dim"))
         else:
             # existing behavior
     ```
     - Updated `render_followup_nudges()` to accept `queue_total` and `plan_start_strict` kwargs
     - When `queue_total > 0` and `plan_start_strict` is set, shows frozen score + queue remaining instead of North Star

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/next.py`** (Modified - Part B4)
     - Added imports for `get_plan_start_strict`, `plan_aware_queue_count`
     - Extracts `plan_start_strict` and `queue_total` from plan data
     - Passes both to `show_empty_queue()` and `render_followup_nudges()`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`** (Modified - Part B5)
     - Added conditional: if plan has plan_start_scores and queue has items, show frozen score; otherwise show existing `score_summary_lines()`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_preflight.py`** (NEW - Part C1)
     - Implements scan queue gate: warns and blocks scan when queue has unfinished items
     - CI profile bypasses gate
     - `--force-rescan` with attestation bypasses and clears plan_start_scores
     - Loads plan, checks plan_start_scores, counts remaining items, gates with sys.exit(1)

   - **`desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py`** (Modified - Part C2)
     - Added `--force-rescan` (store_true) and `--attest` (str) arguments to scan parser

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`** (Modified - Part C3)

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_summary.py`** (Modified - Part D1)
     - Added `_show_score_reveal()` function that reads `state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)` transient key
     - Shows before/after score comparison with visual banner when queue cycle completes
     - Called at top of `show_score_delta()` before normal score output

   - **`desloppify/tests/commands/cli_tests/test_cmd_resolve.py`** (Modified - test fix)
     - Changed assertion from expecting "wontfix items still count against strict score" to `"Scores" in out or "Plan-start score" in out`

   - **`desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_resolve.py`** (Modified - test fix)
     - Same assertion change as above

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **File modified since read error on schema.py**: Got `File has been modified since read` error when trying to edit. Fixed by re-reading the file to get the current on-disk version (which was different from what was initially read due to uncommitted changes in the repo).
   
   - **Pre-existing test collection errors**: `test_plan_resolve_guardrails.py` and `test_epic_synthesis.py` fail to import because they reference `synthesis_phase_banner` and `EPIC_PREFIX` which don't exist in the on-disk schema. These are untracked files not related to our changes.
   
   - **test_wontfix_shows_strict_cost_warning failures (2 files)**: These tests expected the wontfix-specific warning message from `_print_score_movement` which we intentionally replaced with `print_execution_or_reveal`. Fixed by updating both test files to assert for "Scores" or "Plan-start score" instead.
   
   - **test_explain_payload_serializes_item_explain failure**: Expects "Subjective review" in output but the render path changed. Was being investigated when work was interrupted.
   
   - **Other test failures (review_merge, review_batch_core, etc.)**: Appear to be pre-existing failures unrelated to our changes (they test functions like `_build_merge_groups` that don't exist, or `_scored_dimensions_for_lang` with unexpected kwargs).

5. Problem Solving:
   - Solved the cross-phase communication problem for score reveal: Since `merge_scan_results` clears `plan_start_scores` before `show_score_delta` renders, we stash the old values as `state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"]` (transient key, popped during rendering).
   - Solved the "when to snapshot" timing: Snapshot only if `plan_start_scores` is empty (first scan of a new cycle), clear only if queue count is 0.
   - All execution command score replacements follow a consistent pattern: try to load plan, catch exceptions, pass to `print_execution_or_reveal`.

The text IS present in `_render_subjective_task()` which prints `"(Subjective review{tag})"`. The issue may be related to how the rendering path changed or the test's monkeypatching not covering the new code paths.

    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def _show_score_reveal(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    40→    *,
    41→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    """Show before/after score comparison when a queue cycle just completed.
    44→
    45→    This fires when the plan had ``plan_start_scores`` and the queue was empty
    46→    at scan time (i.e. the reconcile block cleared plan_start_scores).  We detect
    47→    this by checking the plan *before* the clear happened — since merge_scan_results
    48→    clears it, we peek at the ``_last_score_reveal`` stash it leaves behind.
    49→    """
    50→    # scan_workflow stashes the old plan_start_scores on state as a transient
    51→    # key when it clears them (queue empty).  Pop it here for the reveal.
    52→    plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
    53→    if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
    54→        return
    55→
    56→    old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
    57→    new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
    58→    delta = round(new_strict - old_strict, 1)
    59→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
    60→
    61→    bar = "=" * 50
    62→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    63→    print(colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold"))
    64→    print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim"))
    65→    print(colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan"))
    66→    if target_strict is not None:
    67→        target_gap = round(target_strict - new_strict, 1)
    68→        if target_gap > 0:
    69→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (+{target_gap:.1f} to go)", "dim"))
    70→        else:
    71→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} — reached!", "green"))
    72→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    73→
    74→
    75→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    76→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    77→    diff_parts = []
    78→    if diff["new"]:
    79→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    80→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    81→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    82→    if diff["reopened"]:
    83→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    84→    if diff_parts:
    85→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    86→    else:
    87→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    88→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    92→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    93→                "yellow",
    94→            )
    95→        )
    96→
    97→
    98→def show_score_delta(
    99→    state: dict,
   100→    prev_overall: float | None,
   101→    prev_objective: float | None,
   102→    prev_strict: float | None,
   103→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
   104→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
   105→    *,
   106→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   107→):
   108→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
   109→    stats = state["stats"]
   110→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   111→
   112→    if (
   113→        new.overall is None
   114→        or new.objective is None
   115→        or new.strict is None
   116→        or new.verified is None
   117→    ):
   118→        print(
   119→            colorize(
   120→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
   121→                "yellow",
   122→            )
   123→        )
   124→        return
   125→
   126→    # Detect score-reveal scan: plan_start_scores existed and queue was clear
   127→    _show_score_reveal(state, new, target_strict=target_strict)
   128→
   129→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
   130→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
   131→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   132→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   133→    print(
   134→        "  Scores: "
   135→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   136→        + colorize(
   137→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   138→            objective_color,
   139→        )
   140→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   141→        + colorize(
   142→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   143→            verified_color,
   144→        )
   145→    )
   146→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   147→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   148→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   149→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   150→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   151→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   152→        print(

   154→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   155→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   156→                "yellow",
   157→            )
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   161→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   162→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   163→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   164→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   165→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   166→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   167→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   168→
   169→    # Show strict target progress
   170→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   171→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
   172→
   173→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   174→
   175→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   176→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   177→        status = integrity.get("status")
   178→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   179→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   180→        if status == "penalized":
   181→            print(
   182→                colorize(
   183→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   184→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   185→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   186→                    "red",
   187→                )
   188→            )
   189→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   190→            if streak >= 2:
   191→                print(
   192→                    colorize(
   193→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   194→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   195→                        "yellow",
   196→                    )
   197→                )
   198→        elif status == "warn":
   199→            print(
   200→                colorize(

   202→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   203→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   204→                    "yellow",
   205→                )
   206→            )
   207→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   208→            if streak >= 2:

   219→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:

   237→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   238→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   239→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)

1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    48→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_replacement_groups(
    52→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    53→    *,
    54→    title: str,
    55→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    58→    if not groups:
    59→        return
    60→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    61→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    62→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    63→        for old, new in replacements:
    64→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    65→    print()
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_ranked_actions(
    69→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    72→    ranked = sorted(
    73→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    74→        key=lambda action: (
    75→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    76→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    77→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    if not ranked:
    81→        return False
    82→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    83→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    84→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    85→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    87→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    88→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    89→        if cluster_count:
    90→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    91→        else:
    92→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    93→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    94→    return True
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = [
    98→    "print_agent_plan",
    99→    "print_ranked_actions",
   100→    "print_replacement_groups",
   101→]
   102→

   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(
   357→    queue: dict,
   358→    tier: int | None,
   359→    strict: float | None,
   360→    *,
   361→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   362→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   363→) -> bool:
   364→    if queue.get("items"):
   365→        return False
   366→    # Show before/after comparison when plan_start_strict is available
   367→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   368→        delta = round(strict - plan_start_strict, 1)
   369→        delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
   370→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   371→        print(colorize(
   372→            f"  Plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Current: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   373→            "cyan",
   374→        ))
   375→        print(colorize(
   376→            "  Run `desloppify scan` to finalize your updated score.",
   377→            "dim",
   378→        ))
   379→    else:
   380→        suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   381→        print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   382→    if tier is not None:
   383→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   384→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   385→        if available:
   386→            commands = " | ".join(
   387→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   388→            )
   389→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   390→    return True
   391→

   393→def render_terminal_items(
   394→    items: list[dict],
   395→    dim_scores: dict,
   396→    findings_scoped: dict,
   397→    *,
   398→    group: str,
   399→    explain: bool,
   400→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   401→    plan: dict | None = None,
   402→) -> None:
   403→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   404→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   405→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   406→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   407→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   408→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   409→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   410→
   411→    if group != "item":
   412→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   413→        return
   414→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   415→        if idx > 0:
   416→            print()
   417→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   418→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   419→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   420→        elif len(items) > 1:
   421→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   422→        else:
   423→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   424→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   425→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   426→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)

   429→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   430→    if len(items) != 1:
   431→        return
   432→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   433→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   434→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   435→        return
   436→    item = items[0]
   437→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   438→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   439→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   440→        primary = item.get(
   441→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   442→        )
   443→        print(f"    {primary}")
   444→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   445→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   449→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   450→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   451→        print(f"    {primary}")
   452→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   453→    else:
   454→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   455→
   456→    print(
   457→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   458→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   459→    )
   460→    print(
   461→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   462→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   463→    )
   464→
   465→
   466→def render_followup_nudges(
   467→    state: dict,
   468→    dim_scores: dict,
   469→    findings_scoped: dict,
   470→    *,
   471→    strict_score: float | None,
   472→    target_strict_score: float,
   473→    queue_total: int = 0,
   474→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   475→) -> None:
   476→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   477→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   478→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   479→        state,
   480→        subjective_entries,
   481→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   482→        max_quality_items=3,
   483→        max_integrity_items=5,
   484→    )
   485→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   486→    # Show frozen plan-start score + queue remaining when in an active cycle
   487→    if queue_total > 0 and plan_start_strict is not None:
   488→        print(
   489→            colorize(
   490→                f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100",
   491→                "cyan",
   492→            )
   493→        )
   494→        print(
   495→            colorize(
   496→                f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   497→                " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.",
   498→                "dim",
   499→            )
   500→        )
   501→    elif strict_score is not None:
   502→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   503→        if gap > 0:
   504→            print(
   505→                colorize(
   506→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   507→                    "cyan",
   508→                )
   509→            )
   510→        else:
   511→            print(
   512→                colorize(
   513→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   514→                    "green",
   515→                )
   516→            )
   517→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   518→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   519→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   520→
   521→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   522→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   523→        findings_scoped
   524→    )
   525→    parts: list[str] = []
   526→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   527→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   528→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   529→    open_review = [
   530→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   531→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   532→    ]
   533→    if low_dims:
   534→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   535→    if stale_count:
   536→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   537→    if unassessed_count:
   538→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   539→    if len(open_review):
   540→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   541→    if coverage_open > 0:
   542→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   543→
   544→    if parts:
   545→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   546→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   547→
   548→
   549→__all__ = [
   550→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   551→    "render_followup_nudges",
   552→    "render_queue_header",
   553→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   554→    "render_terminal_items",
   555→    "scorecard_subjective",
   556→    "show_empty_queue",
   557→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   558→]
   559→

    56→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    57→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""

    68→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    69→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    70→    return bool(
    71→        plan.get("queue_order")
    72→        or plan.get("overrides")
    73→        or plan.get("clusters")
    74→        or plan.get("skipped")

    78→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    79→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    80→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):

    82→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    83→    if recon.resurfaced:
    84→        print(

    87→                "cyan",

   133→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:

   150→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]

   171→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:

   183→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)

   186→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   187→            dirty = True

   196→        if _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   197→            dirty = True

   199→        if dirty:

   253→class ScanRuntime:

   255→

   294→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   295→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   296→    config: dict[str, object],
   297→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   298→    lang: LangRun | None,
   299→) -> LangRun | None:
   300→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   301→    if not lang:
   302→        return None
   303→
   304→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   305→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   306→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   307→        lang,
   308→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   309→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   310→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   311→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   312→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   313→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   314→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   315→        ),
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   319→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   320→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   321→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   322→    }
   323→    return runtime_lang
   324→
   325→
   326→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   327→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   328→    *,
   329→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   330→) -> int:
   331→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   332→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   333→
   334→    reset_keys = {
   335→        key.strip()
   336→        for key in assessments
   337→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   338→    }
   339→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   340→
   341→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   342→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   343→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   344→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   345→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   346→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   347→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   348→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   349→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   350→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   351→            payload.pop("components", None)
   352→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   353→            continue
   354→        assessments[key] = {
   355→            "score": 0.0,
   356→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   357→            "assessed_at": now,
   358→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   359→            "placeholder": True,
   360→        }
   361→    return len(reset_keys)
   362→
   363→
   364→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   365→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   366→) -> int:
   367→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   368→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   369→
   370→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   371→    expired = 0
   372→    for payload in assessments.values():
   373→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   374→            continue
   375→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   376→            continue
   377→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   378→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   379→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   380→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   381→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   382→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   383→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   384→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   385→        payload.pop("components", None)
   386→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   387→        expired += 1
   388→    return expired
   389→
   390→
   391→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   392→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   393→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   394→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   395→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   396→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   397→    path = Path(args.path)
   398→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   399→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   400→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   401→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   402→        state
   403→    )
   404→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   405→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   406→            state,
   407→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   408→        )
   409→
   410→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   411→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   412→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   413→
   414→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   415→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   416→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   417→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   418→
   419→    return ScanRuntime(
   420→        args=args,
   421→        state_path=state_file,
   422→        state=state,
   423→        path=path,
   424→        config=config,
   425→        lang=lang,
   426→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   427→        profile=profile,
   428→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   429→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   430→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   431→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   432→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   433→    )
   434→
   435→
   436→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   437→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   438→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   439→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   440→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   441→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   442→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   443→        return findings
   444→
   445→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   446→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   447→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   448→    )
   449→    if not stale:
   450→        return findings
   451→
   452→    augmented = list(findings)
   453→    augmented.extend(stale)
   454→    for stale_finding in stale:
   455→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   456→    return augmented
   457→
   458→
   459→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   460→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   461→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   462→    *,
   463→    decay_scans: int,
   464→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   465→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   466→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   467→        findings,
   468→        state=runtime.state,
   469→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   470→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   471→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   472→    )
   473→
   474→
   475→def run_scan_generation(
   476→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   477→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   478→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   479→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   480→        disable_parse_cache,
   481→        enable_parse_cache,
   482→    )
   483→
   484→    enable_file_cache()
   485→    enable_parse_cache()
   486→    try:
   487→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   488→            runtime.path,
   489→            lang=runtime.lang,
   490→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   491→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   492→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   493→                profile=runtime.profile,
   494→            ),
   495→        )
   496→    finally:
   497→        disable_parse_cache()
   498→        disable_file_cache()
   499→
   500→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   501→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   502→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   503→    )
   504→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   505→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   506→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   507→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   508→    )
   509→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   510→        findings,
   511→        runtime,
   512→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   513→    )
   514→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   515→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   516→
   517→
   518→def merge_scan_results(
   519→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   520→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   521→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   522→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   523→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   524→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   525→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   526→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   527→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   528→
   529→    if not path_changed:
   530→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   531→    else:
   532→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   533→    prev_dim_scores = (
   534→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   535→    )
   536→
   537→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   538→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   539→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   540→
   541→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   542→
   543→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   544→        runtime.state,
   545→        findings,
   546→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   547→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   548→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   549→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   550→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   551→            potentials=potentials,
   552→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   553→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   554→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   555→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   556→        ),
   557→    )
   558→
   559→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   560→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   561→    )
   562→    state_mod.save_state(
   563→        runtime.state,
   564→        runtime.state_path,
   565→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   566→    )
   567→
   568→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   569→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   570→
   571→    return ScanMergeResult(
   572→        diff=diff,
   573→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   574→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   575→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   576→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   577→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   578→    )
   579→
   580→
   581→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   582→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   583→    config: dict[str, object],
   584→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   585→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   586→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   587→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   588→    )
   589→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   590→    open_findings = [
   591→        finding
   592→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   593→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   594→        ).values()
   595→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   596→    ]
   597→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   598→        open_findings,
   599→        budget=noise_budget,
   600→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   601→    )
   602→
   603→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   604→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   605→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   606→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   607→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   608→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   609→    )
   610→
   611→
   612→def persist_reminder_history(
   613→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   614→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   615→) -> None:
   616→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   617→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   618→        return
   619→
   620→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   621→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   622→    state_mod.save_state(
   623→        runtime.state,
   624→        runtime.state_path,
   625→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   626→    )
   627→
   628→
   629→__all__ = [
   630→    "ScanStateContractError",
   631→    "ScanMergeResult",
   632→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   633→    "ScanRuntime",
   634→    "merge_scan_results",
   635→    "persist_reminder_history",
   636→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   637→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   638→    "run_scan_generation",
   639→]
   640→

1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    69→
    70→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    71→        try:
    72→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    73→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    74→            remaining = 0
    75→        if remaining > 0:
    76→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    77→            return
    78→
    79→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = [
    84→    "get_plan_start_strict",

    39→    )

    41→
    42→

    44→    fixer: FixerConfig,

    47→    total_lines: int,

    50→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    51→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    52→    print(
    53→        colorize(
    54→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    55→            "bold",
    56→        )
    57→    )
    58→    for r in results[:30]:
    59→        syms = ", ".join(r["removed"][:5])
    60→        if len(r["removed"]) > 5:
    61→            syms += f" (+{len(r['removed']) - 5})"
    62→        extra = f"  ({r['lines_removed']} lines)" if r.get("lines_removed") else ""
    63→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")
    64→    if len(results) > 30:
    65→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    66→

    68→def _apply_and_report(

    98→    if skip_reasons is None:

   189→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify fix {fixer_name}"
   190→                )
   191→                resolved_ids.append(fid)
   192→    return resolved_ids

   199→        )

   222→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
   223→    path: Path,
   224→    state: dict,
   225→    _prev_score: float,
   226→    dry_run: bool,
   227→    *,
   228→    lang: LangRun | None = None,

   231→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
   232→        return

   236→    entries = fixer.detect(path)

   243→        results = raw.entries

   245→        results = raw
   246→
   247→    if not results:
   248→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   249→        return
   250→    n_removed = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
   251→    n_lines = sum(r["lines_removed"] for r in results)
   252→    print(

   257→        )
   258→    )
   259→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   260→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   261→    )
   262→    if resolved:

   264→
   265→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup

    55→
    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    57→    """Print the scan header line."""
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_preflight import scan_queue_preflight
   111→
   112→    scan_queue_preflight(args)
   113→    try:
   114→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   115→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   116→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   117→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   118→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   122→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   123→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   124→        runtime,
   125→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   126→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   127→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   128→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   129→    )
   130→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   131→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   132→        print(
   133→            colorize(
   134→                "  * Subjective reset "
   135→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   136→                "yellow",
   137→            )
   138→        )
   139→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   140→        print(
   141→            colorize(
   142→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   143→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   144→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   145→                "yellow",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   149→
   150→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   151→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   152→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   153→
   154→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   155→
   156→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   157→
   158→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   159→    show_score_delta(
   160→        runtime.state,
   161→        merge.prev_overall,
   162→        merge.prev_objective,
   163→        merge.prev_strict,
   164→        merge.prev_verified,
   165→        target_strict=target_value,
   166→    )
   167→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   168→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   169→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   170→
   171→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   172→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   173→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   174→
   175→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   176→        merge.diff,
   177→        runtime.state,
   178→        runtime.lang,
   179→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   180→    )
   181→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   182→
   183→    write_query(
   184→        build_scan_query_payload(
   185→            runtime.state,
   186→            runtime.config,
   187→            runtime.profile,
   188→            merge.diff,
   189→            warnings,
   190→            narrative,
   191→            merge,
   192→            noise,
   193→        ),
   194→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   195→    )
   196→

    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    23→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    24→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    25→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness, check_tool_staleness
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    27→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    28→
    29→
    30→def _scorecard_subjective(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    dim_scores: dict,
    33→) -> list[dict]:
    34→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    35→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→    *,
    42→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    43→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    44→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    45→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    46→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    47→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    48→            continue
    49→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    50→        if strict_val < threshold:
    51→            low.append(
    52→                (
    53→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    54→                    strict_val,
    55→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    56→                )
    57→            )
    58→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    59→    return low
    60→
    61→
    62→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    63→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    64→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    65→    state = runtime.state
    66→    config = runtime.config
    67→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    71→    if stale_warning:
    72→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    73→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    74→    if skill_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    77→    if config_warning:
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    79→
    80→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    84→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    85→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    86→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    87→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    88→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    89→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    90→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    91→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    92→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    93→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    94→
    95→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    96→
    97→    # Load the living plan
    98→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    99→    plan = load_plan()
   100→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   101→    if (
   102→        plan.get("queue_order")
   103→        or plan.get("overrides")
   104→        or plan.get("clusters")
   105→    ):
   106→        plan_data = plan
   107→
   108→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   109→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   110→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   111→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   112→        if active_cluster:
   113→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   114→
   115→    queue = build_work_queue(
   116→        state,
   117→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   118→            tier=tier,
   119→            count=count,
   120→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   121→            scope=scope,
   122→            status=status,
   123→            include_subjective=True,
   124→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   125→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   126→            explain=explain,
   127→            plan=plan_data,
   128→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   129→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   130→        ),
   131→    )
   132→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   133→
   134→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   135→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   136→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   137→        state,
   138→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   139→    )
   140→
   141→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   142→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   143→    )
   144→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   145→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   146→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   147→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   148→        state,
   149→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   150→    )
   151→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   152→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   153→    ]
   154→    write_query(payload)
   155→
   156→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   157→    if output_file:
   158→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   159→            output_file,
   160→            payload,
   161→            len(items),
   162→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   163→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   164→        ):
   165→            return
   166→        raise SystemExit(1)
   167→
   168→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   169→        return
   170→
   171→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue total for lifecycle display
   172→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   173→        get_plan_start_strict,
   174→        plan_aware_queue_count,
   175→    )
   176→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   177→    try:
   178→        queue_total = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan_data) if plan_start_strict is not None else 0
   179→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   180→        queue_total = 0
   181→
   182→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   183→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   184→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   185→        queue,
   186→        tier,
   187→        strict_score,
   188→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   189→        target_strict=target_strict,
   190→    ):
   191→        return
   192→
   193→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   194→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   195→        state.get("findings", {}),
   196→        state.get("scan_path"),
   197→    )
   198→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   199→    try:
   200→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   201→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   202→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   203→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   204→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   205→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   206→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   207→    )
   208→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   209→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   210→        state,
   211→        dim_scores,
   212→        findings_scoped,
   213→        strict_score=strict_score,
   214→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   215→        queue_total=queue_total,
   216→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   217→    )
   218→    print()
   219→
   220→
   221→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   222→

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def print_score_update(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    13→    *,
    14→    config: dict | None = None,
    15→    label: str = "Scores",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    21→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    22→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    23→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    24→    """
    25→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    26→    if (
    27→        new.overall is None
    28→        or new.objective is None
    29→        or new.strict is None
    30→        or new.verified is None
    31→    ):
    32→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    33→        return
    34→
    35→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    36→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    37→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    38→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    39→
    40→    print(
    41→        f"\n  {label}: "
    42→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    43→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    # Always show strict target + next-command nudge
    49→    if config is None:
    50→        from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    51→
    52→        config = config_mod.load_config()
    53→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    54→
    55→    target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    56→    _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _print_strict_target_nudge(
    60→    strict: float, target: float, *, show_next: bool = True,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Print a one-liner with strict→target and optional next-command nudge."""
    63→    gap = round(target - strict, 1)
    64→    if gap > 0:
    65→        suffix = " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement" if show_next else ""
    66→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}){suffix}", "dim"))
    67→    else:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!", "green"))
    69→
    70→
    71→__all__ = ["print_score_update", "_print_strict_target_nudge"]
    72→

1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    12→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    13→    # CI profile always passes
    14→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    15→        return
    16→
    17→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    18→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    19→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    20→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    21→            print(
    22→                colorize(
    23→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    24→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    25→                    'completion"',
    26→                    "red",
    27→                ),
    28→                file=sys.stderr,
    29→            )
    30→            sys.exit(1)
    31→        print(
    32→            colorize(
    33→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    34→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    35→                "yellow",
    36→            )
    37→        )
    38→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    39→        try:
    40→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    41→
    42→            plan = load_plan()
    43→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    44→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    45→                save_plan(plan)
    46→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    47→            _ = exc
    48→        return
    49→
    50→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    51→    try:
    52→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    53→
    54→        plan = load_plan()
    55→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    56→        return
    57→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return  # No active cycle
    59→
    60→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    61→    try:
    62→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
    63→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    64→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    65→            plan_aware_queue_count,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    69→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    70→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    71→        return
    72→    if remaining == 0:
    73→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    74→
    75→    # GATE
    76→    print(
    77→        colorize(
    78→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    79→            " remaining in your queue.",
    80→            "red",
    81→        ),
    82→        file=sys.stderr,
    83→    )
    84→    print(
    85→        colorize(
    86→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    87→            "red",
    88→        ),
    89→        file=sys.stderr,
    90→    )
    91→    print(
    92→        colorize(
    93→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    94→            "dim",
    95→        ),
    96→        file=sys.stderr,
    97→    )
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
   101→            "dim",
   102→        ),
   103→        file=sys.stderr,
   104→    )
   105→    print(
   106→        colorize(
   107→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   108→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   109→            'completion"',
   110→            "yellow",
   111→        ),
   112→        file=sys.stderr,
   113→    )
   114→    sys.exit(1)
