Excellent! Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile the full agent-visible lifecycle output:

## Complete Desloppify Agent Lifecycle: Exact Output Strings

I've traced the full lifecycle of a desloppify session from an agent's perspective. Here are the exact strings an agent would see at each stage:

---

### **STAGE 1: Initial Scan (`cmd_scan`)**

**Entry point:** `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py::cmd_scan()`

**Output sequence:**

```
1. Scan header:
   colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold")
   → "\nDesloppify Scan (python)\n"

2. Subjective reset warning (if applicable):
   colorize("  * Subjective reset {reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0", "yellow")

3. Expired manual-override warning (if applicable):
   colorize("  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: {count} dimension(s) reset to 0. Use trusted review...", "yellow")

4. Coverage preflight warnings (if any):
   colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow")

5. Scan completion banner:
   colorize("  Scan complete", "bold")
   colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim")

6. Diff summary (from scan_reporting_summary.py::show_diff_summary):
   colorize(f"+{new_count} new", "yellow")           # if any new
   colorize(f"-{auto_resolved_count} resolved", "green")  # if any resolved
   colorize(f"↻{reopened_count} reopened", "red")    # if any reopened
   # or:
   colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim")   # if no changes
   
   If suspect detectors (returned 0, likely transient):
   colorize("  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: {detectors} (returned 0 — likely transient)", "yellow")

7. Score delta block (from scan_reporting_summary.py::show_score_delta):
   
   7a. If queue-cycle complete (score reveal):
       colorize(f"  {'=' * 50}", "cyan")
       colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold")
       colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim")
       colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan")
       # If target_strict provided:
       colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} to go)", "dim")  # or "reached!"
       colorize(f"  {'=' * 50}", "cyan")
   
   7b. Live score display:
       "  Scores: " + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{delta}", overall_color) + 
                      colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{delta}", objective_color) + 
                      colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{delta}", strict_color) + 
                      colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{delta}", verified_color)
   
   7c. If non-comparable:
       colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {reason}", "yellow")
   
   7d. If wontfix debt significant (gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10):
       colorize(f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — {wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt", "yellow")
   
   7e. Score legend (on first scan or gap > 10 or agent environment):
       colorize("  Score guide:", "dim")
       colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim")
       colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim")
       colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim")
       colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim")
   
   7f. Strict target progress (if target_strict set):
       colorize(f"  ⚠ strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} to go)", "yellow")
   
   7g. Subjective integrity penalties/warnings:
       colorize("  ⚠ Subjective integrity: {matched} dimension matched {target}. Re-review recommended.", "yellow")
       # or:
       colorize("  ⚠ Subjective integrity: {matched} target-matched dimensions reset to 0.0 {target}.", "red")

8. Scan visibility hints:
   colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow")  # if fast scan
   colorize(f"  * {budget_warning}", "yellow")  # if noise budget warning
   colorize(f"  * {hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise_budget}/detector)...", "dim")  # if hidden

9. Scorecard subjective measures (if applicable)

10. Score model breakdown (if applicable)

11. Dimension deltas (if previous dimensions existed)

12. Post-scan analysis warnings and narrative
    (custom coaching based on dimension health)

13. Concern count (if any):
    colorize(f"  {count} potential design concern(s) (run `show concerns` to view)", "cyan")
```

---

### **STAGE 2: Running `next` command with queue available**

**Entry point:** `/desloppify/app/commands/next.py::cmd_next()` → `_get_items()`

**Output sequence:**

```
1. Warnings (if any):
   colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow")      # tool staleness
   colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow")      # skill version mismatch
   colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow")     # config staleness

2. Tier navigator header:
   colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold")
   colorize(f"    Open: T1:{counts[1]} T2:{counts[2]} T3:{counts[3]} T4:{counts[4]}", "dim")
   colorize("    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | ... ", "dim")

3. Single item display (if count=1):
   colorize("  [#123]", "bold")  # or just "[Next item]"
   colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim")
   colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold")
   f"  {colorize(summary, 'yellow')}"
   f"  Type: {type_label}"  # e.g. "Design review (requires judgment)" or "Auto-fixable"
   f"  File: {file}"
   colorize(f"  ID:   {id}", "dim")
   f"  Lines: {line_list}"
   f"  Category: {category}"
   
   For dimension display:
   colorize(f"\n  Dimension: {dimension_name} — {score:.1f}% (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) ({issues}/{checks} checks failing)", "dim")
   
   For score impact:
   colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan")
   
   For auto-fixable similar items:
   colorize(f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. Run `{command}` to fix all at once.", "cyan")

4. Resolution hints:
   colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim")
   f'    desloppify plan done "{item_id}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item_id}" --note "<why>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'

5. Frozen queue progress (if plan_start_strict exists):
   colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100", "cyan")
   colorize(f"  Queue: {queue_total} item(s) remaining — complete the queue, then scan...", "dim")

6. Strict target progress (if no active plan cycle):
   colorize(f"\n  North star: strict {strict:.1f}/100 → target {target:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)", "cyan")
   # or:
   colorize(f"\n  North star: strict {strict:.1f}/100 meets target {target:.1f}", "green")

7. Subjective followup nudges:
   colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {parts_summary}.", "cyan")
   colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim")
```

---

### **STAGE 3: After Fix command (real fix, not dry-run)**

**Entry point:** `/desloppify/app/commands/fix/cmd.py::cmd_fix()` → `apply_flow.py::_apply_and_report()`

**Output sequence:**

```
1. Detection phase:
   colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer_label}...", "dim")   # stderr
   colorize(f"  Found {count} {label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim")  # stderr

2. Fix summary:
   colorize(f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n", "bold")
   # Examples of changes:
   f"  {rel(file)}{extra}  →  {symbols}"

3. Auto-resolve notification:
   f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} findings in state"

4. Frozen score (if plan_start_scores exists and queue not empty):
   colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100", "cyan")
   colorize(f"  Queue: {queue_total} item(s) remaining — complete the queue, then scan...", "dim")

5. Or live score update (if no frozen score):
   f"\n  Scores: " + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{delta}", color) + ... 
   colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}) — run `desloppify next`...", "dim")

6. Post-fix check summary:
   colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim")
   colorize(f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} findings resolved)", "dim")
   
   If skip reasons:
   colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim")
   colorize(f"    {count:4d}  {reason_label}", "dim")

7. If cascading import cleanup:
   colorize("  Cascade: removed {n_removed} now-orphaned imports from {file_count} files ({n_lines} lines)", "green")
   f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import findings"
```

---

### **STAGE 4: After Resolve command (when resolving findings)**

**Entry point:** `/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py::cmd_resolve()`

**Output sequence:**

```
1. If no findings matched:
   colorize(f"No {status_label} findings matching: {patterns}", "yellow")

2. If resolved:
   (From _print_resolve_summary)
   colorize(f"  Resolved {count} finding(s)", "green")  # custom output

3. Plan queue update (if living plan exists):
   colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim")

4. Wontfix batch warning (if applicable):
   colorize(f"  ⚠ {count} wontfix findings resolved...", "yellow")

5. Frozen score or live score (same as fix command):
   colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100", "cyan")
   # or:
   print_score_update(state, prev)

6. Milestone achievement (if any):
   colorize(f"  → {milestone_text}", "green")
```

---

### **STAGE 5: When queue empties during `next` command**

**Entry point:** `/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py::show_empty_queue()`

**Output sequence:**

```
1. If plan_start_strict is available:
   colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green")
   colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Current: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}", "cyan")
   colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to finalize your updated score.", "dim")

2. If no plan:
   colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green")  # suffix = strict score if available

3. If tier was requested but unavailable:
   colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim")
   colorize(f"  Try: {tier_commands}", "dim")
```

---

### **STAGE 6: Scanning post-queue-clear (plan-start-scores clear triggered)**

**Entry point:** `scan_workflow.py::_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` and `scan_reporting_summary.py::_show_score_reveal()`

**When triggered:**
- `scan_workflow.py` detects `queue_empty == True` with `plan_start_scores` set
- Stashes old scores in `state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"]`
- Clears `plan["plan_start_scores"]` to `{}`
- Calls `scan_reporting_summary.py::show_score_delta()` which pops the stashed scores

**Output:**

```
The "SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!" block is shown:

colorize(f"  {'=' * 50}", "cyan")
colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold")
colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim")
colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan")
# If target_strict provided:
colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (reached!)", "green")  # or "({gap:.1f} to go)"
colorize(f"  {'=' * 50}", "cyan")
```

Then the rest of the scan summary follows (diff, live scores, etc.).

---

### **STAGE 7: When scanning with unfinished queue (preflight gate)**

**Entry point:** `scan_preflight.py::scan_queue_preflight()`

**Output (on stderr):**

```
colorize(f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item(s) remaining in your queue.", "red")
colorize("  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.", "red")
colorize("  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.", "dim")
colorize("\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):", "dim")
colorize('    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not the intended workflow..."', "yellow")
[Exit 1]
```

If `--force-rescan` with valid attestation:
```
colorize("  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. Plan-start score will be reset.", "yellow")
```

---

### **Key Color Codes Used:**

| Color | Usage |
|-------|-------|
| **bold** | Section headers, major findings, fix summaries |
| **cyan** | Score updates, next-item labels, dimension insights, plan state |
| **green** | Positive changes, resolved items, achievements, target reached |
| **yellow** | Warnings, skipped items, non-comparable data, action nudges |
| **red** | Critical warnings, penalties, gating failures |
| **dim** | Supporting details, explanations, suggestions, secondary info |

---

### **Score Lifecycle:**

1. **Initial scan**: Shows all 4 scores (overall, objective, strict, verified) with deltas from previous
2. **After fix/resolve (queue still active)**: Shows **frozen plan-start score** + queue count (no live score update)
3. **After resolve/fix (queue empty)**: Shows **live score update** with target nudge
4. **When queue cleared**: Shows **frozen vs. updated** comparison in "SCORE UPDATE" block
5. **Next scan after queue cycle**: Shows `_show_score_reveal()` block, then live scores, then clears plan_start_scores

---

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement a "Frozen Plan-Start Score + Queue Execution Lifecycle" feature for the desloppify Python CLI tool. The implementation was completed in a previous session. In this session, the user's requests were:
   - "once you're done, can you sense-check the whole thing holistically? Is it excellently engineered? Is it robust? Is there any way you can actually run through all the various scenarios?" (from previous session, carried forward)
   - "Can you try to simulate a real world scenario and just try to understand what an agent would think at each point?"
   - "and is it clear at all points that to update the strict score they need to work through the entire plan?"

   The lifecycle concept: scan → plan → execute queue (fix/resolve/next) → scan (score reveal) → repeat. During execution, scores are frozen to the plan-start snapshot. Scanning mid-queue is gated with attestation required to bypass.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Frozen plan-start score snapshots stored in `plan.json` as `plan_start_scores`
   - Plan-aware queue counting (respecting skips, collapsing clusters)
   - Scan queue gate (preflight guard modeled on review/preflight.py pattern)
   - Attestation-based bypass for force-rescan
   - Transient state stashing (`_plan_start_scores_for_reveal`) for cross-phase communication within a single scan run
   - `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS` tuple: `(ImportError, AttributeError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError)` for consistent error handling
   - `QueueBuildOptions` dataclass for configuring work queue builds
   - `ScoreSnapshot` named tuple from `state_mod`
   - Circular import avoidance via local imports (queue_progress.py ↔ scan_workflow.py chain)
   - Python import caching: `from X import Y` creates a local binding; monkeypatching the module attribute doesn't affect existing bindings

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`** (NEW - core helper)
     - Central module for the entire frozen score feature
     - Contains `plan_aware_queue_count()`, `get_plan_start_strict()`, `print_frozen_score_with_queue_context()`, `print_execution_or_reveal()`
     - CRITICAL: imports must be LOCAL to avoid circular dependency chain
     ```python
     """Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     from __future__ import annotations
     from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

     def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
         from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
         result = build_work_queue(
             state,
             options=QueueBuildOptions(
                 status="open", count=None, plan=plan,
                 collapse_clusters=True, include_skipped=False,
             ),
         )
         return result["total"]

     def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
         if not plan:
             return None
         return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")

     def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan: dict, queue_remaining: int) -> None:
         scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
         strict = scores.get("strict")
         if strict is None:
             return
         print(colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100", "cyan"))
         print(colorize(
             f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
             " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.", "dim",
         ))

     def print_execution_or_reveal(state: dict, prev, plan: dict | None) -> None:
         if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
             try:
                 remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
             except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
                 remaining = 0
             if remaining > 0:
                 print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
                 return
         from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
         print_score_update(state, prev)
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_preflight.py`** (NEW - scan gate)
     - Implements scan queue gate: warns/blocks scan when queue has unfinished items
     - CI profile bypasses; --force-rescan with attestation bypasses and clears plan_start_scores
     ```python
     def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
         if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
             return
         if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
             attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
             if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
                 # ... error message ...
                 sys.exit(1)
             # Clear plan_start_scores
             try:
                 plan = load_plan()
                 if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
                     plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
                     save_plan(plan)
             except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
                 _ = exc
             return
         try:
             plan = load_plan()
         except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
             return
         if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
             return
         try:
             from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
             state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
             remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
         except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
             return
         if remaining == 0:
             return
         # GATE - print warning to stderr, sys.exit(1)
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`** (Modified - snapshot + clear + bug fix)
     - Added `_seed_plan_start_scores()` and `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` functions
     - Fixed set-then-immediately-clear bug with `seeded` guard
     - Moved `plan_aware_queue_count` import from top-level to local (circular import fix)
     ```python
     def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state):
         if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
             return False
         scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
         if scores.strict is None:
             return False
         plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
             "strict": scores.strict, "overall": scores.overall,
             "objective": scores.objective, "verified": scores.verified,
         }
         return True

     def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan):
         if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
             return False
         try:
             from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
             queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
         except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
             _ = exc
             return False
         if not queue_empty:
             return False
         state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
         plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
         return True

     # In _reconcile_plan_post_scan:
         seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
         if seeded:
             dirty = True
         # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass
         if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
             dirty = True
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_summary.py`** (Modified - score reveal)
     - Cleaned up verbose 25-line comment block to 2 lines
     - Removed unnecessary `load_plan()` call and unused `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS` import
     ```python
     def _show_score_reveal(state, new, *, target_strict=None):
         # scan_workflow stashes the old plan_start_scores on state as a transient
         # key when it clears them (queue empty).  Pop it here for the reveal.
         plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
         if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
             return
         old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
         new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
         # ... banner display ...
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`** (Modified - lifecycle nudge)

         from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan_for_nudge
         _scan_plan = _load_plan_for_nudge()

             from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
             _q = plan_aware_queue_count(runtime.state, _scan_plan)
             if _q > 0:
                 print(colorize(
                     f"  Workflow: {_q} queue item{'s' if _q != 1 else ''}."
                     " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Replaced `print_score_update` with `print_execution_or_reveal`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/merge.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Replaced `print_score_update` with `print_execution_or_reveal`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Updated `show_empty_queue()` with `plan_start_strict` and `target_strict` kwargs
     - Updated `render_followup_nudges()` with `queue_total` and `plan_start_strict` kwargs

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/next.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Extracts `plan_start_strict` and `queue_total` from plan data
     - Passes both to `show_empty_queue()` and `render_followup_nudges()`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Conditional frozen/live score display

   - **`desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py`** (Modified - previous session)
     - Added `--force-rescan` and `--attest` arguments to scan parser

   - **Test files modified:**
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_scan.py` - Added scan_preflight_mod import + monkeypatch mocks for all 3 tests
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/cli_tests/test_cmd_scan.py` - Same changes
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_next.py` - Added plan_mod import + `load_plan` mock on BOTH plan_mod AND next_mod
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/cli_tests/test_cmd_next.py` - Same changes
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/test_cmd_resolve.py` - Updated wontfix assertion (previous session)
     - `desloppify/tests/commands/review_tests/test_review_preflight.py` - Updated assertions for changed message format and changed test names (import_skips_preflight → import_calls_preflight, external_submit_skips_preflight → external_submit_calls_preflight)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Scan tests hitting real plan.json**: scan_queue_preflight was loading a real plan.json from the project directory. Fixed by adding `monkeypatch.setattr(scan_preflight_mod, "scan_queue_preflight", lambda _: None)` to each test.
   
   - **Next tests hitting real plan.json**: `load_plan()` imported at module level in next.py meant monkeypatching `plan_mod.load_plan` wasn't enough - the local binding in next.py still pointed to the real function. Fixed by patching BOTH `plan_mod.load_plan` AND `next_mod.load_plan`.
   
   - **Circular import**: A linter hoisted local imports in `queue_progress.py` to top-level, creating a circular dependency chain: `scan_workflow.py → queue_progress.py → score_update.py → scan_helpers.py → scan/__init__.py → scan.py → scan_artifacts.py → scan_workflow.py`. Fixed by moving `build_work_queue` and `print_score_update` imports back to local imports inside their respective functions, and moving `plan_aware_queue_count` import in scan_workflow.py to local.
   
   - **Set-then-immediately-clear bug**: `_seed_plan_start_scores` and `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty` both ran sequentially in the same reconcile pass. When queue was empty, block 1 would set plan_start_scores and block 2 would immediately clear them, causing a spurious "Queue cycle complete!" reveal with 0 delta. Fixed by adding `seeded` flag: `if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(...)`.
   
   - **Verbose comment block**: `_show_score_reveal` had a 25-line stream-of-consciousness comment. Cleaned to 2 lines.
   
   - **Unnecessary load_plan call**: `_show_score_reveal` loaded the plan just to check it existed, but the transient stash key was sufficient. Removed.
   
   - **Unused import**: `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS` was left behind in `scan_reporting_summary.py` after removing the try/except that used it. Removed.
   
   - **Inconsistent exception handling**: `scan_preflight.py` used `(OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError)` instead of `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS`. Changed to use the shared tuple.
   
   - **Review preflight test assertions**: Pre-existing message format changes in review/preflight.py caused test failures. Updated test assertions and renamed tests to match new behavior (import and external_submit now call preflight).
   
   - **test_visualize failures**: Pre-existing import hoisting in visualize.py (not from our changes) caused 2 new test failures. Not fixed as they're outside our scope.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Used `git stash` to establish a clean baseline of pre-existing failures (15 failures). Compared against our changes to identify which failures were new.
   - Identified that Python's `from X import Y` creates a local binding that isn't affected by monkeypatching the module's attribute - need to patch both the module AND any local bindings.
   - Traced the full agent lifecycle step by step to identify the set-then-clear bug and the messaging gap.
   - Final test results: 4016 passed, 10 failed (8 pre-existing + 2 from pre-existing visualize import hoist), 3 skipped. Zero new failures from our feature.

6. All user messages:

   - "Can you try to simulate a real world scenario and just try to understand what an agent would think at each point?"
   - "and is it clear at all points that to update the strict score they need to work through the entire plan?"

7. Pending Tasks:
   - The implementation is complete and tests pass. The holistic review was done. The lifecycle simulation was performed. The messaging clarity question was addressed by adding a scan lifecycle nudge.
   - No explicitly pending tasks remain from the user's requests.

8. Current Work:
   I had just finished addressing the user's question "is it clear at all points that to update the strict score they need to work through the entire plan?" by:
   1. Auditing all output messages across every command surface (fix, resolve, next, status, scan)
   2. Identifying a gap: when scan first establishes plan_start_scores, nothing in the scan output tells the agent about the frozen lifecycle
   3. Adding a lifecycle nudge in `cmd_scan` (scan.py) that prints after show_score_delta:
      ```
      Workflow: 14 queue items. Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.
      ```
   4. Verifying tests pass (140 passed on targeted tests, 4016 passed on full suite)
   5. Providing a complete walkthrough of what the agent sees at every point

9. Optional Next Step:
   The user's questions have been addressed. The feature is implemented, tested, and reviewed. No explicit next step was requested. If the user wants, they could:
   - Commit the changes
   - Write dedicated tests for the new scan preflight and lifecycle features
   - Test the feature end-to-end on a real project

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    69→        try:
    70→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    71→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    72→            remaining = 0
    73→        if remaining > 0:
    74→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    75→            return
    76→
    77→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    78→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    79→
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = [
    84→    "get_plan_start_strict",
    85→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
    86→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
    87→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
    88→]
    89→

1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    10→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    13→
    14→
    15→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    16→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    17→    # CI profile always passes
    18→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    19→        return
    20→
    21→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    22→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    23→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    24→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    25→            print(
    26→                colorize(
    27→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    28→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    29→                    'completion"',
    30→                    "red",
    31→                ),
    32→                file=sys.stderr,
    33→            )
    34→            sys.exit(1)
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    38→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    39→                "yellow",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    43→        try:
    44→            plan = load_plan()
    45→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    46→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    47→                save_plan(plan)
    48→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    49→            _ = exc
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    53→    try:
    54→        plan = load_plan()
    55→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    56→        return
    57→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return  # No active cycle
    59→
    60→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    61→    try:
    62→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    63→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    64→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    65→        return
    66→    if remaining == 0:
    67→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    68→
    69→    # GATE
    70→    print(
    71→        colorize(
    72→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    73→            " remaining in your queue.",
    74→            "red",
    75→        ),
    76→        file=sys.stderr,
    77→    )
    78→    print(
    79→        colorize(
    80→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    81→            "red",
    82→        ),
    83→        file=sys.stderr,
    84→    )
    85→    print(
    86→        colorize(
    87→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    88→            "dim",
    89→        ),
    90→        file=sys.stderr,
    91→    )
    92→    print(
    93→        colorize(
    94→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
    95→            "dim",
    96→        ),
    97→        file=sys.stderr,
    98→    )
    99→    print(
   100→        colorize(
   101→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   102→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   103→            'completion"',
   104→            "yellow",
   105→        ),
   106→        file=sys.stderr,
   107→    )
   108→    sys.exit(1)
   109→
   110→
   111→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   112→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    70→
    71→
    72→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    73→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    74→    now = utc_now()
    75→    return {
    76→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    77→        "created": now,
    78→        "updated": now,
    79→        "queue_order": [],
    80→        "deferred": [],
    81→        "skipped": {},
    82→        "active_cluster": None,
    83→        "overrides": {},
    84→        "clusters": {},
    85→        "superseded": {},
    86→        "plan_start_scores": {},
    87→    }
    88→
    89→
    90→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    91→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    92→
    93→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    94→    """
    95→    defaults = empty_plan()
    96→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    97→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    98→
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   100→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   102→        plan["deferred"] = []
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   104→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   106→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   108→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   109→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   110→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   111→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
   112→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   113→
   114→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   115→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   116→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   117→    if deferred:
   118→        now = utc_now()
   119→        for fid in list(deferred):
   120→            if fid not in skipped:
   121→                skipped[fid] = {
   122→                    "finding_id": fid,
   123→                    "kind": "temporary",
   124→                    "reason": None,
   125→                    "note": None,
   126→                    "attestation": None,
   127→                    "created_at": now,
   128→                    "review_after": None,
   129→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   130→                }
   131→        deferred.clear()
   132→
   133→    # Normalize cluster fields
   134→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   135→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   136→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   137→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   138→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   139→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   140→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   141→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   142→
   143→
   144→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   145→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   146→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   147→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   148→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   149→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   150→
   151→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   152→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   153→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   154→    if overlap:
   155→        raise ValueError(
   156→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   157→        )
   158→
   159→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   160→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   161→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   162→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   163→            raise ValueError(
   164→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   165→            )
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "PLAN_VERSION",

    71→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    72→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    73→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    74→        return
    75→    try:
    76→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    77→        _save_config(config)
    78→    except OSError as exc:
    79→        _ = exc
    80→        config["needs_rescan"] = True

    83→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    84→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    85→    return bool(
    86→        plan.get("queue_order")
    87→        or plan.get("overrides")
    88→        or plan.get("clusters")
    89→        or plan.get("skipped")
    90→    )

    93→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    94→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    95→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    96→        return False
    97→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    98→    if recon.resurfaced:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   102→                "cyan",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→    return bool(recon.changes)
   106→

   108→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   109→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""

   111→    if sync.injected:
   112→        print(
   113→            colorize(
   114→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   115→                "cyan",

   121→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   122→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   123→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   124→    if sync.pruned:
   125→        print(

   127→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",

   135→                "cyan",

   142→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""

   146→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   147→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   148→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   149→        return False
   150→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   151→    if scores.strict is None:
   152→        return False
   153→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   154→        "strict": scores.strict,
   155→        "overall": scores.overall,
   156→        "objective": scores.objective,
   157→        "verified": scores.verified,
   158→    }
   159→    return True
   160→
   161→
   162→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   163→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   164→) -> bool:
   165→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   166→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   167→        return False
   168→
   169→    try:
   170→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   171→
   172→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   173→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   174→        _ = exc
   175→        return False
   176→    if not queue_empty:
   177→        return False
   178→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   179→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   180→    return True

   183→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   184→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   185→    try:
   186→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   187→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   188→        dirty = False
   189→
   190→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   191→            dirty = True
   192→        if _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions):
   193→            dirty = True
   194→        if _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions):
   195→            dirty = True
   196→        if _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state):
   197→            dirty = True
   198→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   199→        if seeded:
   200→            dirty = True
   201→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   202→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   203→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   204→            dirty = True
   205→
   206→        if dirty:
   207→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   208→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   209→        _ = exc

   255→@dataclass
   256→class ScanRuntime:
   257→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   258→

   296→
   297→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   298→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   299→    config: dict[str, object],
   300→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   301→    lang: LangRun | None,
   302→) -> LangRun | None:
   303→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   304→    if not lang:
   305→        return None
   306→
   307→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   308→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   309→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   310→        lang,
   311→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   312→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   313→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   314→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   315→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   316→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   317→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   318→        ),
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   322→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   323→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   324→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   325→    }
   326→    return runtime_lang
   327→
   328→
   329→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   330→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   331→    *,
   332→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   333→) -> int:
   334→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   335→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   336→
   337→    reset_keys = {
   338→        key.strip()
   339→        for key in assessments
   340→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   341→    }
   342→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   343→
   344→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   345→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   346→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   347→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   348→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   349→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   350→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   351→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   352→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   353→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   354→            payload.pop("components", None)
   355→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   356→            continue
   357→        assessments[key] = {
   358→            "score": 0.0,
   359→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   360→            "assessed_at": now,
   361→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   362→            "placeholder": True,
   363→        }
   364→    return len(reset_keys)
   365→
   366→
   367→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   368→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   369→) -> int:
   370→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   371→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   372→
   373→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   374→    expired = 0
   375→    for payload in assessments.values():
   376→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   377→            continue
   378→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   379→            continue
   380→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   381→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   382→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   383→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   384→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   385→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   386→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   387→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   388→        payload.pop("components", None)
   389→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   390→        expired += 1
   391→    return expired
   392→
   393→
   394→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   395→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   396→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   397→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   398→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   399→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   400→    path = Path(args.path)
   401→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   402→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   403→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   404→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   405→        state
   406→    )
   407→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   408→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   409→            state,
   410→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   411→        )
   412→
   413→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   414→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   415→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   416→
   417→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   418→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   419→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   420→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   421→
   422→    return ScanRuntime(
   423→        args=args,
   424→        state_path=state_file,
   425→        state=state,
   426→        path=path,
   427→        config=config,
   428→        lang=lang,
   429→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   430→        profile=profile,
   431→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   432→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   433→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   434→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   435→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   440→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   441→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   442→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   443→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   444→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   445→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   446→        return findings
   447→
   448→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   449→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   450→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   451→    )
   452→    if not stale:
   453→        return findings
   454→
   455→    augmented = list(findings)
   456→    augmented.extend(stale)
   457→    for stale_finding in stale:
   458→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   459→    return augmented
   460→
   461→
   462→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   463→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   464→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   465→    *,
   466→    decay_scans: int,
   467→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   468→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   469→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   470→        findings,
   471→        state=runtime.state,
   472→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   473→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   474→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   475→    )
   476→
   477→
   478→def run_scan_generation(
   479→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   480→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   481→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   482→    enable_file_cache()
   483→    enable_parse_cache()
   484→    try:
   485→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   486→            runtime.path,
   487→            lang=runtime.lang,
   488→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   489→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   490→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   491→                profile=runtime.profile,
   492→            ),
   493→        )
   494→    finally:
   495→        disable_parse_cache()
   496→        disable_file_cache()
   497→
   498→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   499→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   500→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   501→    )
   502→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   503→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   504→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   505→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   506→    )
   507→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   508→        findings,
   509→        runtime,
   510→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   511→    )
   512→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   513→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   514→
   515→
   516→def merge_scan_results(
   517→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   518→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   519→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   520→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   521→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   522→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   523→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   524→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   525→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   526→
   527→    if not path_changed:
   528→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   529→    else:
   530→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   531→    prev_dim_scores = (
   532→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   536→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   537→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   538→
   539→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   540→
   541→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   542→        runtime.state,
   543→        findings,
   544→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   545→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   546→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   547→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   548→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   549→            potentials=potentials,
   550→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   551→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   552→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   553→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   554→        ),
   555→    )
   556→
   557→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   558→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   559→    )
   560→    state_mod.save_state(
   561→        runtime.state,
   562→        runtime.state_path,
   563→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   564→    )
   565→
   566→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   567→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   568→
   569→    return ScanMergeResult(
   570→        diff=diff,
   571→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   572→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   573→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   574→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   575→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   576→    )
   577→
   578→
   579→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   580→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   581→    config: dict[str, object],
   582→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   583→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   584→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   585→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   586→    )
   587→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   588→    open_findings = [
   589→        finding
   590→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   591→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   592→        ).values()
   593→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   594→    ]
   595→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   596→        open_findings,
   597→        budget=noise_budget,
   598→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   602→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   603→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   604→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   605→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   606→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   607→    )
   608→
   609→
   610→def persist_reminder_history(
   611→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   612→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   613→) -> None:
   614→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   615→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   616→        return
   617→
   618→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   619→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   620→    state_mod.save_state(
   621→        runtime.state,
   622→        runtime.state_path,
   623→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   624→    )
   625→
   626→
   627→__all__ = [
   628→    "ScanStateContractError",
   629→    "ScanMergeResult",
   630→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   631→    "ScanRuntime",
   632→    "merge_scan_results",
   633→    "persist_reminder_history",
   634→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   635→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   636→    "run_scan_generation",
   637→]
   638→

1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    21→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    22→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    23→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    24→        return 0
    25→
    26→    streak = 0
    27→    for entry in reversed(history):
    28→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    29→            break
    30→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    31→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    32→            break
    33→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    34→            break
    35→        streak += 1
    36→    return streak
    37→
    38→
    39→def _show_score_reveal(
    40→    state: dict,
    41→    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    42→    *,
    43→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    44→) -> None:
    45→    """Show before/after score comparison when a queue cycle just completed.
    46→
    47→    This fires when the plan had ``plan_start_scores`` and the queue was empty
    48→    at scan time (i.e. the reconcile block cleared plan_start_scores).  We detect
    49→    this by checking the plan *before* the clear happened — since merge_scan_results
    50→    clears it, we peek at the ``_last_score_reveal`` stash it leaves behind.
    51→    """
    52→    # scan_workflow stashes the old plan_start_scores on state as a transient
    53→    # key when it clears them (queue empty).  Pop it here for the reveal.
    54→    plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
    55→    if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
    56→        return
    57→
    58→    old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
    59→    new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
    60→    delta = round(new_strict - old_strict, 1)
    61→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
    62→
    63→    bar = "=" * 50
    64→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    65→    print(colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold"))
    66→    print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim"))
    67→    print(colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan"))
    68→    if target_strict is not None:
    69→        target_gap = round(target_strict - new_strict, 1)
    70→        if target_gap > 0:
    71→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (+{target_gap:.1f} to go)", "dim"))
    72→        else:
    73→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} — reached!", "green"))
    74→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    75→
    76→
    77→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    78→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    79→    diff_parts = []
    80→    if diff["new"]:
    81→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    82→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    83→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    84→    if diff["reopened"]:
    85→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    86→    if diff_parts:
    87→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    88→    else:
    89→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    90→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    94→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    95→                "yellow",
    96→            )
    97→        )
    98→
    99→
   100→def show_score_delta(
   101→    state: dict,
   102→    prev_overall: float | None,
   103→    prev_objective: float | None,
   104→    prev_strict: float | None,
   105→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
   106→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
   107→    *,
   108→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   109→):
   110→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
   111→    stats = state["stats"]
   112→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   113→
   114→    if (
   115→        new.overall is None
   116→        or new.objective is None
   117→        or new.strict is None
   118→        or new.verified is None
   119→    ):
   120→        print(
   121→            colorize(
   122→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
   123→                "yellow",
   124→            )
   125→        )
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    # Detect score-reveal scan: plan_start_scores existed and queue was clear
   129→    _show_score_reveal(state, new, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
   132→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
   133→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   134→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   135→    print(
   136→        "  Scores: "
   137→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   138→        + colorize(
   139→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   140→            objective_color,
   141→        )
   142→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   143→        + colorize(
   144→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   145→            verified_color,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   149→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   150→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   151→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   152→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   153→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   154→        print(
   155→            colorize(
   156→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   157→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   158→                "yellow",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   165→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   166→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   167→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   168→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   169→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   170→
   171→    # Show strict target progress
   172→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   173→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   174→
   175→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   176→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   177→        status = integrity.get("status")
   178→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   179→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   180→        if status == "penalized":
   181→            print(
   182→                colorize(
   183→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   184→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   185→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   186→                    "red",
   187→                )
   188→            )
   189→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   190→            if streak >= 2:
   191→                print(
   192→                    colorize(
   193→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   194→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   195→                        "yellow",
   196→                    )
   197→                )
   198→        elif status == "warn":
   199→            print(
   200→                colorize(
   201→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   202→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   203→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   204→                    "yellow",
   205→                )
   206→            )
   207→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   208→            if streak >= 2:
   209→                print(
   210→                    colorize(
   211→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   212→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   213→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   214→                        "yellow",
   215→                )
   216→            )
   217→
   218→
   219→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   220→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   221→    try:
   222→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   223→        if concerns:
   224→            print(
   225→                colorize(
   226→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   227→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   228→                    "cyan",
   229→                )
   230→            )
   231→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   232→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   233→
   234→
   235→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   236→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   237→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   238→    for message, style in lines:
   239→        print(colorize(message, style))
   240→    return target, gap
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   244→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import query_file_path

    45→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (
    46→    ScanStateContractError,

    53→from desloppify.core.query import write_query

    55→
    56→
    57→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    58→    """Print the scan header line."""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    60→
    61→
    62→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    63→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    64→    lines = [
    65→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    66→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    67→    ]
    68→    print("\n".join(lines))
    69→
    70→
    71→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    72→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   111→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   112→    try:
   113→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   114→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   115→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   116→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   117→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   118→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   119→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   120→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   121→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   122→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   123→        runtime,
   124→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   125→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   126→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   127→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   128→    )
   129→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   130→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   131→        print(
   132→            colorize(
   133→                "  * Subjective reset "
   134→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   135→                "yellow",
   136→            )
   137→        )
   138→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   139→        print(
   140→            colorize(
   141→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   142→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   143→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   144→                "yellow",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   148→
   149→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   150→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   151→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   152→
   153→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   154→
   155→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   156→
   157→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   158→    show_score_delta(
   159→        runtime.state,
   160→        merge.prev_overall,
   161→        merge.prev_objective,
   162→        merge.prev_strict,
   163→        merge.prev_verified,
   164→        target_strict=target_value,
   165→    )
   166→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle
   167→    try:
   168→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan_for_nudge
   169→        _scan_plan = _load_plan_for_nudge()
   170→        if _scan_plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   171→            from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   172→            _q = plan_aware_queue_count(runtime.state, _scan_plan)
   173→            if _q > 0:
   174→                print(colorize(
   175→                    f"  Workflow: {_q} queue item{'s' if _q != 1 else ''}."
   176→                    " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
   177→                    "dim",
   178→                ))
   179→    except Exception:
   180→        pass
   181→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   182→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   183→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   184→
   185→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   186→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   187→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   188→
   189→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   190→        merge.diff,
   191→        runtime.state,
   192→        runtime.lang,
   193→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   194→    )
   195→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   196→
   197→    write_query(
   198→        build_scan_query_payload(
   199→            runtime.state,
