```
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import print_execution_or_reveal
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    15→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import list_open_review_findings
    16→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    17→from desloppify.state import save_state, utc_now
    18→
    19→
    20→def _word_set(text: str) -> set[str]:
    21→    words = "".join(ch.lower() if ch.isalnum() else " " for ch in text).split()
    22→    return {word for word in words if len(word) >= 3}
    23→
    24→
    25→def _summary_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    26→    left = _word_set(a)
    27→    right = _word_set(b)
    28→    if not left or not right:
    29→        return 0.0
    30→    overlap = len(left & right)
    31→    union = len(left | right)
    32→    return float(overlap) / float(union) if union else 0.0
    33→
    34→
    35→def _parse_holistic_identifier(finding_id: str) -> str:
    36→    parts = [part for part in str(finding_id).split("::") if part]
    37→    if len(parts) < 2:
    38→        return ""
    39→    candidate = parts[-2].strip()
    40→    if not candidate or candidate in {"holistic", "changed", "stale", "unreviewed"}:
    41→        return ""
    42→    return candidate
    43→
    44→
    45→def _related_files_set(finding: dict) -> set[str]:
    46→    related = finding.get("detail", {}).get("related_files", [])
    47→    if not isinstance(related, list):
    48→        return set()
    49→    return {str(path).strip() for path in related if str(path).strip()}
    50→
```

```
    48→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    49→    """Show score dashboard."""
    50→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    51→    state = runtime.state
    52→    config = runtime.config
    53→
    54→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    55→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    56→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    57→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    58→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    59→
    60→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    61→        print(
    62→            json.dumps(
    63→                _status_json_payload(
    64→                    state,
    65→                    stats,
    66→                    dim_scores,
    67→                    scorecard_dims,
    68→                    subjective_measures,
    69→                    suppression,
    70→                ),
    71→                indent=2,
    72→            )
    73→        )
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    80→    if stale_warning:
    81→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    82→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    83→    if skill_warning:
    84→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    85→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    86→    if config_warning:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    88→
    89→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    90→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    91→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→

    94→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    95→

    97→    _plan = load_plan()
    98→    _plan_active = _plan if (
    99→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   100→    ) else None
   101→
   102→    narrative = compute_narrative(

   105→    )
   106→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
```

```
╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                       CODE REFERENCE & SNIPPETS                           ║
╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

1. QUEUE PROGRESS HELPERS (queue_progress.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    result = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            count=None,
            plan=plan,
            collapse_clusters=True,
            include_skipped=False,
        ),
    )
    return result["total"]


def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    if not plan:
        return None
    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")


def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan: dict, queue_remaining: int) -> None:
    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    strict = scores.get("strict")
    if strict is None:
        return
    print(colorize(f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100", "cyan"))
    print(
        colorize(
            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
            " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.",
            "dim",
        )
    )


def print_execution_or_reveal(state: dict, prev, plan: dict | None) -> None:
    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores."""
    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
        try:
            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
            remaining = 0
        if remaining > 0:
            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
            return
    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    print_score_update(state, prev)


2. SCAN PREFLIGHT GATE (scan_preflight.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    # CI profile always passes
    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
        return
    
    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
            print(colorize('  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand..."', "red"))
            sys.exit(1)
        # Clear plan_start_scores
        try:
            plan = load_plan()
            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
                save_plan(plan)
        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
            pass
        return
    
    # No plan = no gate
    try:
        plan = load_plan()
    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
        return
    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
        return  # No active cycle
    
    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    try:
        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
        return
    if remaining == 0:
        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    
    # GATE - exit with error
    print(colorize(f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item(s) remaining in your queue.", "red"))
    print(colorize("  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.", "red"))
    sys.exit(1)


3. PLAN SCHEMA (schema.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    version: Required[int]
    created: Required[str]
    updated: Required[str]
    queue_order: list[str]
    deferred: list[str]
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    active_cluster: str | None
    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle


def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    now = utc_now()
    return {
        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
        "created": now,
        "updated": now,
        "queue_order": [],
        "deferred": [],
        "skipped": {},
        "active_cluster": None,
        "overrides": {},
        "clusters": {},
        "superseded": {},
        "plan_start_scores": {},  # Empty initially
    }


4. SCAN WORKFLOW RECONCILIATION (scan_workflow.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], 
                             state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
        return False  # Already seeded, no-op
    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    if scores.strict is None:
        return False
    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
        "strict": scores.strict,
        "overall": scores.overall,
        "objective": scores.objective,
        "verified": scores.verified,
    }
    return True  # Seeded!


def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
) -> bool:
    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
        return False
    
    try:
        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
        return False
    if not queue_empty:
        return False
    # Stash for later reveal display
    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    return True


def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
    try:
        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
        dirty = False
        
        # ... other reconciliations ...
        
        # CRITICAL: Capture seeded flag BEFORE attempting clear
        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
        if seeded:
            dirty = True
        
        # GUARD: Only clear scores that existed BEFORE this reconcile pass
        # Never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan
        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
            dirty = True
        
        if dirty:
            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
        pass


5. SCORE REVEAL (scan_reporting_summary.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _show_score_reveal(
    state: dict,
    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    *,
    target_strict: float | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Show before/after score comparison when a queue cycle just completed."""
    # Pop the stashed plan_start_scores (cleared by scan_workflow)
    plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
    if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
        return
    
    old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
    new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
    delta = round(new_strict - old_strict, 1)
    
    bar = "=" * 50
    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    print(colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold"))
    print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim"))
    print(colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan"))
    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))


def show_score_delta(
    state: dict,
    prev_overall: float | None,
    prev_objective: float | None,
    prev_strict: float | None,
    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    *,
    target_strict: float | None = None,
):
    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    
    # Detect score-reveal scan: plan_start_scores existed and queue was clear
    _show_score_reveal(state, new, target_strict=target_strict)
    
    # ... rest of score display ...


6. SCAN COMMAND LIFECYCLE (scan.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
    try:
        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan_for_nudge
        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
        return
    
    try:
        plan = _load_plan_for_nudge()
        if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
            return
        queue_total = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        return
    
    if queue_total <= 0:
        return
    print(
        colorize(
            f"  Workflow: {queue_total} queue item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}."
            " Score is frozen until the queue is clear — use `desloppify next` to begin.",
            "dim",
        )
    )


def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)  # GATE 1
    
    # ... prepare runtime ...
    
    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
    
    # ... show_diff_summary ...
    
    show_score_delta(...)  # Calls _show_score_reveal() internally
    
    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about lifecycle
    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)  # COACHING


7. STATUS COMMAND (status_cmd.py)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Show score dashboard."""
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    config = runtime.config
    
    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering
    _plan = load_plan()
    _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None
    
    # Show frozen plan-start score when in an active queue cycle
    _plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(_plan)
    _status_queue_remaining = 0
    if _plan_start_strict is not None:
        try:
            _status_queue_remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, _plan)
        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
            _status_queue_remaining = 0
    
    if _plan_start_strict is not None and _status_queue_remaining > 0:
        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(_plan, _status_queue_remaining)
    else:
        # Show live scores
        for line, style in score_summary_lines(...):
            print(colorize(line, style))


8. OTHER COMMANDS (resolve/fix/review)
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    _plan = None

prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
print_execution_or_reveal(runtime.state, prev, _plan)
# Shows frozen score if cycle active, otherwise live scores
```

```
LIFECYCLE FLOW VERIFICATION
═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Phase 1: QUEUE CREATION (First Scan)
─────────────────────────────────────
User: desloppify plan create --from-scan
  → scan_workflow._reconcile_plan_post_scan() runs
  → _seed_plan_start_scores() captures baseline scores
  → plan["plan_start_scores"] = {strict: 75.0, overall: 80.0, ...}
  → Result: Frozen baseline snapshot

Phase 2: PREFLIGHT GATE (Subsequent Scans)
──────────────────────────────────────────
User: desloppify scan
  → scan_preflight.scan_queue_preflight(args) called first
  → Checks: plan_start_scores exists AND queue_count > 0
  → If yes: GATE with error message
  → Result: User forced to complete queue before rescanning

Phase 3: QUEUE EXECUTION (Multiple Commands)
─────────────────────────────────────────────
User: desloppify next/fix/resolve/review ...
  → Each command uses print_execution_or_reveal()
  → Shows: "Plan-start score: strict 75.0/100"
  →        "Queue: 5 items remaining — complete the queue..."
  → Result: Continuous visibility of frozen score + progress

Phase 4: SCAN AFTER QUEUE CLEAR (Final Scan)
──────────────────────────────────────────────
User: desloppify scan (with queue empty)
  → scan_preflight.scan_queue_preflight() passes (queue empty)
  → Detectors run normally
  → _reconcile_plan_post_scan() runs:
    - seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores() returns False (already set)
    - if not seeded: _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty() runs
    - Stashes old scores: state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"]
    - Clears: plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}

Phase 5: SCORE REVEAL (Scan Reporting)
──────────────────────────────────────
  → show_score_delta() calls _show_score_reveal()
  → Pops _plan_start_scores_for_reveal from state
  → Displays:
    =====================================
    SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!
    Plan-start: strict 75.0/100
    Updated:    strict 78.5/100 (+3.5)
    Target:     95.0 (+16.5 to go)
    =====================================
  → Result: User sees their cycle improvement

Phase 6: STATUS FEEDBACK (Ongoing)
──────────────────────────────────
User: desloppify status
  → status_cmd checks get_plan_start_strict()
  → If plan_start_scores empty: shows live scores (cycle complete)
  → Result: New baseline established for next cycle

EDGE CASES HANDLED
═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

1. Multiple scans without queue completion
   → Preflight gate blocks all (except CI/force-rescan)

2. Queue with auto-clustered items
   → plan_aware_queue_count collapses clusters
   → Reports accurate remaining count

3. User clears queue manually (via skip/resolve)
   → Next scan sees queue_empty = True
   → Clears scores and shows reveal

4. Excluded findings affect queue
   → plan_aware_queue_count respects exclusions
   → Queue count accurate for user experience

5. Plan file missing/corrupted
   → All operations wrapped in try/except
   → Gracefully falls back to normal flow
   → Never crashes scan

6. Schema migration (adding plan_start_scores)
   → ensure_plan_defaults() handles missing field
```
